+        }
+
         public fun onCancelCountdown() {
             countdownJob?.cancel()
             countdownJob = null
             stopRecording()
+            wipeStaleSosFile()
             _sosUiState.value = SosUiState.Idle
         }
 
         /** Fires the SOS immediately, cancelling the countdown. Used by the "Send Now" button. */
         public fun onSendNow() {
             countdownJob?.cancel()
             countdownJob = null
             stopRecording()
             viewModelScope.launch { fireSos() }
         }
 
         /** Called by the UI after the RECORD_AUDIO OS permission dialog resolves. */
         public fun onAudioPermissionResult(granted: Boolean) {
             startCountdown(audioGranted = granted)
         }
 
         private fun startCountdown(audioGranted: Boolean) {
             val osPermissionGranted =
                 ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(context, Manifest.permission.RECORD_AUDIO) ==
                     PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
             if (audioGranted && !osPermissionGranted) {
                 // Pause and ask UI to request the OS permission; countdown resumes via onAudioPermissionResult.
                 _sosUiState.value = SosUiState.RequestAudioPermission
                 return
             }
             countdownJob?.cancel()
             countdownJob =
                 viewModelScope.launch {
+                    // Wipe any stale file from a crash/old build before this countdown starts.
+                    wipeStaleSosFile()
+                    freshRecordingCaptured = false
                     if (audioGranted && osPermissionGranted) startRecording()
                     for (sec in 30 downTo 1) {
                         _sosUiState.value = SosUiState.Countdown(sec)
                         delay(1_000L)
                     }
                     stopRecording()
                     fireSos()
                 }
         }
 
         private fun startRecording() {
             runCatching {
                 val dir = File(context.filesDir, "sos").also { it.mkdirs() }
                 val file = File(dir, "sos-$bookingId.m4a")
                 val rec =
                     if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
                         MediaRecorder(context)
                     } else {
                         @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                         MediaRecorder()
                     }
                 rec.apply {
                     setAudioSource(MediaRecorder.AudioSource.MIC)
                     setOutputFormat(MediaRecorder.OutputFormat.MPEG_4)
                     setAudioEncoder(MediaRecorder.AudioEncoder.AAC)
                     setOutputFile(file.absolutePath)
                     prepare()
                     start()
                 }
                 recorder = rec
+                freshRecordingCaptured = true
             }
         }

 134: @Composable
 135: private fun SosOverlay(
 136:     sosUiState: SosUiState,
 137:     sosViewModel: SosViewModel,
 138:     snackbarHostState: SnackbarHostState,
 139: ) {
 140:     val sosConfirmedMsg = stringResource(R.string.tracking_sos_confirmed)
 141:     val sosErrorMsg = stringResource(R.string.tracking_sos_error)
 142:     when (val sos = sosUiState) {
 143:         is SosUiState.ShowConsent ->
 144:             SosConsentDialog(
 145:                 onGranted = { sosViewModel.onConsentResolved(true) },
 146:                 onDenied = { sosViewModel.onConsentResolved(false) },
 147:             )
 148:         is SosUiState.Countdown ->
 149:             SosBottomSheet(
 150:                 secondsLeft = sos.secondsLeft,
 151:                 onCancel = { sosViewModel.onCancelCountdown() },
 152:                 onConfirmNow = { sosViewModel.onSendNow() },
 153:             )
 154:         is SosUiState.SosConfirmed -> {
 155:             LaunchedEffect(sos) { snackbarHostState.showSnackbar(sosConfirmedMsg) }
 156:         }
 157:         is SosUiState.SosError -> {
 158:             LaunchedEffect(sos) { snackbarHostState.showSnackbar(sosErrorMsg) }
 159:         }
 160:         is SosUiState.UploadingEvidence ->
 161:             SosUploadingEvidenceSheet(pct = sos.pct, onDismiss = {})
 162:         is SosUiState.EvidenceSaved ->
 163:             SosEvidenceSavedSheet(onDismiss = { sosViewModel.onDismissEvidenceResult() })
 164:         is SosUiState.EvidenceUploadError ->
 165:             SosEvidenceUploadErrorSheet(message = sos.message, onDismiss = { sosViewModel.onDismissEvidenceResult() })
 166:         else -> Unit
 167:     }
 168: }

 326: @Composable
 327: private fun StatusTimeline(currentStatus: BookingStatus) {
 328:     val stages =
 329:         listOf(
 330:             BookingStatus.EnRoute to stringResource(R.string.status_en_route),
 331:             BookingStatus.Reached to stringResource(R.string.status_reached),
 332:             BookingStatus.InProgress to stringResource(R.string.status_in_progress),
 333:             BookingStatus.Completed to stringResource(R.string.status_done),
 334:         )
 335:     val activeIndex = stages.indexOfFirst { (status, _) -> status == currentStatus }
 336: 
 337:     stages.forEachIndexed { index, (_, label) ->
 338:         val isActive = activeIndex >= 0 && index <= activeIndex
 339:         Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically, modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 3.dp)) {
 340:             Surface(
 341:                 modifier = Modifier.size(if (isActive) 14.dp else 10.dp),
 342:                 shape = MaterialTheme.shapes.extraSmall,
 343:                 color =
 344:                     if (isActive) {
 345:                         MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary
 346:                     } else {
 347:                         MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surfaceVariant
 348:                     },
 349:             ) {}
 350:             Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
 351:             Text(
 352:                 text = label,
 353:                 style = if (isActive) MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium else MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
 354:                 color = if (isActive) MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
 355:             )
 356:         }
 357:     }
 358: }
 359: 
 360: // NOTE: statusLabel is a non-composable function retained for backward-compat with callers
 361: // that cannot access stringResource. Composable callers (like TrackingBody) should use
 362: // the composable overload above (StatusTimeline / label chip) instead.
 363: // TODO(E12-S02a): convert status enum labels fully in E18 when all callers are composable.
 364: private fun statusLabel(status: BookingStatus): String =
 365:     when (status) {
 366:         BookingStatus.PendingPayment -> "Payment pending"
 367:         BookingStatus.Paid -> "Booking confirmed"
 368:         BookingStatus.Searching -> "Finding technician"
 369:         BookingStatus.Assigned -> "Technician assigned"
 370:         BookingStatus.EnRoute -> "Technician on the way"
 371:         BookingStatus.Reached -> "Technician arrived"
 372:         BookingStatus.InProgress -> "Work in progress"
 373:         BookingStatus.AwaitingPriceApproval -> "Price approval needed"
 374:         BookingStatus.Completed -> "Service completed"
 375:         BookingStatus.Closed -> "Booking closed"
 376:         BookingStatus.Cancelled -> "Booking cancelled"
 377:         BookingStatus.Unfulfilled -> "Technician unavailable"
 378:         BookingStatus.Unknown -> "Status unavailable"
 379:     }

  13: import com.homeservices.customer.data.sos.SosAudioUploader

  17: import com.homeservices.customer.domain.flags.FeatureFlags
  18: import com.homeservices.customer.domain.sos.SosUseCase
  19: import dagger.hilt.android.lifecycle.HiltViewModel
  20: import dagger.hilt.android.qualifiers.ApplicationContext
  21: import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
  22: import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
  23: import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
  24: import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow

  27: import java.io.File
  28: import javax.inject.Inject
  29: 
  30: @HiltViewModel
  31: @Suppress("LongParameterList", "TooManyFunctions")
  32: public class SosViewModel
  33:     @Inject
  34:     constructor(
  35:         savedStateHandle: SavedStateHandle,
  36:         private val sosUseCase: SosUseCase,
  37:         private val consentStore: SosConsentStore,
  38:         @ApplicationContext private val context: Context,
  39:         private val featureFlags: FeatureFlags,
  40:         private val sessionManager: SessionManager,
  41:         private val audioUploader: SosAudioUploader,
  42:     ) : ViewModel() {
  43:         private val bookingId: String = checkNotNull(savedStateHandle["bookingId"])
  44:         private val _sosUiState = MutableStateFlow<SosUiState>(SosUiState.Idle)
  45:         public val sosUiState: StateFlow<SosUiState> = _sosUiState.asStateFlow()
  46: 
  47:         private var countdownJob: Job? = null
  48:         private var recorder: MediaRecorder? = null
  49: 
  50:         /** Set to true only when startRecording() completes successfully in this SOS session. */
  51:         private var freshRecordingCaptured = false
  52: 
  53:         public fun onSosTapped() {
  54:             viewModelScope.launch {
  55:                 val consent = consentStore.getAudioConsent()
  56:                 if (consent == null) {
  57:                     _sosUiState.value = SosUiState.ShowConsent
  58:                 } else {
  59:                     startCountdown(audioGranted = consent)
  60:                 }
  61:             }
  62:         }
  63: 
  64:         public fun onConsentResolved(granted: Boolean) {
  65:             viewModelScope.launch {
  66:                 consentStore.setAudioConsent(granted)
  67:                 startCountdown(audioGranted = granted)
  68:             }
  69:         }
  70: 
  71:         /** Dismiss the evidence-saved or evidence-error sheet after upload completes. */
  72:         public fun onDismissEvidenceResult() {
  73:             _sosUiState.value = SosUiState.SosConfirmed
  74:         }
  75: 
  76:         public fun onCancelCountdown() {
  77:             countdownJob?.cancel()
  78:             countdownJob = null
  79:             stopRecording()
  80:             wipeStaleSosFile()
  81:             _sosUiState.value = SosUiState.Idle
  82:         }
  83: 
  84:         /** Fires the SOS immediately, cancelling the countdown. Used by the "Send Now" button. */
  85:         public fun onSendNow() {
  86:             countdownJob?.cancel()
  87:             countdownJob = null
  88:             stopRecording()
  89:             viewModelScope.launch { fireSos() }
  90:         }
  91: 
  92:         /** Called by the UI after the RECORD_AUDIO OS permission dialog resolves. */
  93:         public fun onAudioPermissionResult(granted: Boolean) {
  94:             startCountdown(audioGranted = granted)
  95:         }
  96: 
  97:         private fun startCountdown(audioGranted: Boolean) {
  98:             val osPermissionGranted =
  99:                 ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(context, Manifest.permission.RECORD_AUDIO) ==
 100:                     PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
 101:             if (audioGranted && !osPermissionGranted) {
 102:                 // Pause and ask UI to request the OS permission; countdown resumes via onAudioPermissionResult.
 103:                 _sosUiState.value = SosUiState.RequestAudioPermission
 104:                 return
 105:             }
 106:             countdownJob?.cancel()
 107:             countdownJob =
 108:                 viewModelScope.launch {
 109:                     // Wipe any stale file from a crash/old build before this countdown starts.
 110:                     wipeStaleSosFile()
 111:                     freshRecordingCaptured = false
 112:                     if (audioGranted && osPermissionGranted) startRecording()
 113:                     for (sec in 30 downTo 1) {
 114:                         _sosUiState.value = SosUiState.Countdown(sec)
 115:                         delay(1_000L)
 116:                     }
 117:                     stopRecording()
 118:                     fireSos()
 119:                 }
 120:         }
 121: 
 122:         private fun startRecording() {
 123:             runCatching {
 124:                 val dir = File(context.filesDir, "sos").also { it.mkdirs() }
 125:                 val file = File(dir, "sos-$bookingId.m4a")
 126:                 val rec =
 127:                     if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
 128:                         MediaRecorder(context)
 129:                     } else {
 130:                         @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
 131:                         MediaRecorder()
 132:                     }
 133:                 rec.apply {
 134:                     setAudioSource(MediaRecorder.AudioSource.MIC)
 135:                     setOutputFormat(MediaRecorder.OutputFormat.MPEG_4)
 136:                     setAudioEncoder(MediaRecorder.AudioEncoder.AAC)
 137:                     setOutputFile(file.absolutePath)
 138:                     prepare()
 139:                     start()
 140:                 }
 141:                 recorder = rec
 142:                 freshRecordingCaptured = true
 143:             }
 144:         }
 145: 
 146:         /** For testing only: simulate a successful recording capture. */
 147:         @Suppress("unused")
 148:         internal fun simulateFreshRecordingCapturedForTest() {
 149:             freshRecordingCaptured = true
 150:         }
 151: 
 152:         private fun stopRecording() {
 153:             runCatching {
 154:                 recorder?.apply {
 155:                     stop()
 156:                     release()
 157:                 }
 158:                 recorder = null
 159:             }
 160:         }
 161: 
 162:         private suspend fun fireSos() {
 163:             val result = sosUseCase.execute(bookingId)
 164:             if (!result.isSuccess) {
 165:                 _sosUiState.value = SosUiState.SosError(result.exceptionOrNull()?.message ?: "Unknown error")
 166:                 return
 167:             }
 168:             _sosUiState.value = SosUiState.SosConfirmed
 169:             maybeUploadEvidence()
 170:         }
 171: 
 172:         private suspend fun maybeUploadEvidence() {
 173:             if (!featureFlags.sosAudioUploadEnabled() || !freshRecordingCaptured) return
 174:             val customerId = (sessionManager.authState.value as? AuthState.Authenticated)?.uid
 175:             val file = File(File(context.filesDir, "sos"), "sos-$bookingId.m4a")
 176:             if (customerId == null || !file.exists()) return
 177: 
 178:             val bytes = file.readBytes()
 179:             file.delete()
 180:             audioUploader.upload(customerId, bookingId, bytes).collect { progress ->
 181:                 _sosUiState.value =
 182:                     when (progress) {
 183:                         is SosUploadProgress.Progress -> SosUiState.UploadingEvidence(progress.pct)
 184:                         is SosUploadProgress.Success -> SosUiState.EvidenceSaved
 185:                         is SosUploadProgress.Failure ->
 186:                             SosUiState.EvidenceUploadError(progress.cause.message ?: "upload_failed")
 187:                     }
 188:             }
 189:         }
 190: 
 191:         private fun wipeStaleSosFile() {
 192:             freshRecordingCaptured = false
 193:             runCatching { File(File(context.filesDir, "sos"), "sos-$bookingId.m4a").delete() }
 194:         }
 195: 
 196:         override fun onCleared() {
 197:             super.onCleared()
 198:             countdownJob?.cancel()
 199:             stopRecording()
 200:             wipeStaleSosFile()
 201:         }
 202:     }
--- customer-app/app/src/main/kotlin/com/homeservices/customer/data/sos/SosAudioUploader.kt
   1: package com.homeservices.customer.data.sos
   2: 
   3: import com.google.firebase.storage.FirebaseStorage
   4: import com.homeservices.customer.data.sos.remote.SosApiService
   5: import com.homeservices.customer.data.sos.remote.SosKeyUploadRequest
   6: import kotlinx.coroutines.channels.awaitClose
   7: import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
   8: import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
   9: import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.callbackFlow
  10: import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
  11: import javax.inject.Inject
  12: 
  13: public sealed interface SosUploadProgress {
  14:     public data class Progress(
  15:         public val pct: Int,
  16:     ) : SosUploadProgress
  17: 
  18:     public data object Success : SosUploadProgress
  19: 
  20:     public data class Failure(
  21:         public val cause: Throwable,
  22:     ) : SosUploadProgress
  23: }
  24: 
  25: public class SosAudioUploader
  26:     @Inject
  27:     constructor(
  28:         private val storage: FirebaseStorage,
  29:         private val sosApi: SosApiService,
  30:         private val cipher: SosAudioCipher,
  31:     ) {
  32:         private companion object {
  33:             const val PCT_MAX = 100
  34:             const val KEY_POST_RETRIES = 3
  35:             const val KEY_POST_RETRY_DELAY_MS = 2_000L
  36:         }
  37: 
  38:         public fun upload(
  39:             customerId: String,
  40:             incidentId: String,
  41:             plaintextAudio: ByteArray,
  42:         ): Flow<SosUploadProgress> =
  43:             callbackFlow {
  44:                 val encrypted = cipher.encrypt(plaintextAudio)
  45:                 plaintextAudio.fill(0)
  46: 
  47:                 val path = "sos-audio/$customerId/$incidentId.enc"
  48:                 val task = storage.reference.child(path).putBytes(encrypted.ciphertext)
  49:                 val scope = this
  50: 
  51:                 task.addOnProgressListener { snapshot ->
  52:                     val total = snapshot.totalByteCount
  53:                     val pct = if (total > 0) ((snapshot.bytesTransferred * PCT_MAX) / total).toInt() else 0
  54:                     trySend(SosUploadProgress.Progress(pct))
  55:                 }
  56: 
  57:                 task.addOnFailureListener { e ->
  58:                     trySend(SosUploadProgress.Failure(e))
  59:                     close()
  60:                 }
  61: 
  62:                 task.addOnSuccessListener {
  63:                     scope.launch {
  64:                         var lastError: Throwable? = null
  65:                         repeat(KEY_POST_RETRIES) { attempt ->
  66:                             if (attempt > 0) delay(KEY_POST_RETRY_DELAY_MS)
  67:                             runCatching {
  68:                                 sosApi.uploadKey(
  69:                                     incidentId,
  70:                                     SosKeyUploadRequest(encrypted.keyB64, encrypted.ivB64, path),
  71:                                 )
  72:                             }.onSuccess {
  73:                                 trySend(SosUploadProgress.Success)
  74:                                 close()
  75:                                 return@launch
  76:                             }.onFailure { e -> lastError = e }
  77:                         }
  78:                         trySend(SosUploadProgress.Failure(lastError!!))
  79:                         close()
  80:                     }
  81:                 }
  82: 
  83:                 awaitClose {
  84:                     if (!task.isComplete) task.cancel()
  85:                 }
  86:             }
  87:     }
--- customer-app/app/src/main/kotlin/com/homeservices/customer/data/sos/SosAudioCipher.kt
   1: package com.homeservices.customer.data.sos
   2: 
   3: import java.security.SecureRandom
   4: import java.util.Base64
   5: import javax.crypto.Cipher
   6: import javax.crypto.KeyGenerator
   7: import javax.crypto.spec.GCMParameterSpec
   8: import javax.inject.Inject
   9: 
  10: public data class EncryptedBlob(
  11:     public val ciphertext: ByteArray,
  12:     public val keyB64: String,
  13:     public val ivB64: String,
  14: ) {
  15:     override fun equals(other: Any?): Boolean {
  16:         if (this === other) return true
  17:         if (other !is EncryptedBlob) return false
  18:         return ciphertext.contentEquals(other.ciphertext) && keyB64 == other.keyB64 && ivB64 == other.ivB64
  19:     }
  20: 
  21:     override fun hashCode(): Int = 31 * ciphertext.contentHashCode() + keyB64.hashCode() + ivB64.hashCode()
  22: }
  23: 
  24: public class SosAudioCipher
  25:     @Inject
  26:     constructor() {
  27:         private val random = SecureRandom()
  28:         private val encoder = Base64.getEncoder()
  29: 
  30:         private companion object {
  31:             const val AES_KEY_BITS = 256
  32:             const val GCM_IV_BYTES = 12
  33:             const val GCM_TAG_BITS = 128
  34:         }
  35: 
  36:         public fun encrypt(plaintext: ByteArray): EncryptedBlob {
  37:             val key = KeyGenerator.getInstance("AES").apply { init(AES_KEY_BITS) }.generateKey()
  38:             val iv = ByteArray(GCM_IV_BYTES).also { random.nextBytes(it) }
  39: 
  40:             val cipher = Cipher.getInstance("AES/GCM/NoPadding")
  41:             cipher.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, key, GCMParameterSpec(GCM_TAG_BITS, iv))
  42:             val ciphertext = cipher.doFinal(plaintext)
  43: 
  44:             return EncryptedBlob(
  45:                 ciphertext = ciphertext,
  46:                 keyB64 = encoder.encodeToString(key.encoded),
  47:                 ivB64 = encoder.encodeToString(iv),
  48:             )
  49:         }
  50:     }
--- api/src/functions/admin/sos/playback-token.ts
   1: import '../../../bootstrap.js';
   2: import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
   3: import { app } from '@azure/functions';
   4: import type { HttpHandler, InvocationContext } from '@azure/functions';
   5: import { requireAdmin } from '../../../middleware/requireAdmin.js';
   6: import type { AdminHttpHandler } from '../../../middleware/requireAdmin.js';
   7: import type { AdminContext } from '../../../types/admin.js';
   8: import { bookingRepo } from '../../../cosmos/booking-repository.js';
   9: import { getKeyDoc } from '../../../cosmos/sos-incident-key-repository.js';
  10: import { getSessionById } from '../../../services/adminSession.service.js';
  11: import { assertTotpFreshness } from '../../../services/totp.service.js';
  12: import { getFirebaseAdmin } from '../../../services/firebaseAdmin.js';
  13: import { appendAuditEntry } from '../../../cosmos/audit-log-repository.js';
  14: import type { AuditLogDoc } from '../../../schemas/audit-log.js';
  15: 
  16: const TOTP_MAX_AGE_MS = 5 * 60_000;
  17: const ALLOWED_ROLES = ['super-admin', 'ops-manager'] as const;
  18: 
  19: export const adminSosPlaybackTokenHandler: AdminHttpHandler = async (
  20:   req,
  21:   ctx: InvocationContext,
  22:   admin: AdminContext,
  23: ) => {
  24:   if (!ALLOWED_ROLES.includes(admin.role as (typeof ALLOWED_ROLES)[number])) {
  25:     return { status: 403, jsonBody: { code: 'FORBIDDEN' } };
  26:   }
  27: 
  28:   const session = await getSessionById(admin.sessionId);
  29:   if (!session || !assertTotpFreshness(session, TOTP_MAX_AGE_MS)) {
  30:     return { status: 401, jsonBody: { code: 'TOTP_REFRESH_REQUIRED' } };
  31:   }
  32: 
  33:   const incidentId = (req as unknown as { params: { incidentId: string } }).params.incidentId;
  34: 
  35:   const booking = await bookingRepo.getById(incidentId);
  36:   if (!booking) return { status: 404, jsonBody: { code: 'BOOKING_NOT_FOUND' } };
  37: 
  38:   const keyDoc = await getKeyDoc(incidentId, booking.customerId);
  39:   if (!keyDoc) return { status: 404, jsonBody: { code: 'KEY_NOT_FOUND' } };
  40: 
  41:   const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + 5 * 60_000);
  42:   const [signedStorageUrl] = await getFirebaseAdmin()
  43:     .storage()
  44:     .bucket()
  45:     .file(keyDoc.storagePath)
  46:     .getSignedUrl({ action: 'read', expires: expiresAt });
  47: 
  48:   const now = new Date().toISOString();
  49:   const auditEntry: AuditLogDoc = {
  50:     id: randomUUID(),
  51:     adminId: admin.adminId,
  52:     role: admin.role,
  53:     action: 'SOS_PLAYBACK_TOKEN_ISSUED',
  54:     resourceType: 'booking',
  55:     resourceId: incidentId,
  56:     payload: { adminId: admin.adminId, incidentId },
  57:     timestamp: now,
  58:     partitionKey: now.slice(0, 7),
  59:   };
  60:   appendAuditEntry(auditEntry).catch((err: unknown) => ctx.error('Audit SOS_PLAYBACK_TOKEN_ISSUED failed', err));
  61: 
  62:   return {
  63:     status: 200,
  64:     jsonBody: {
  65:       incidentId,
  66:       storagePath: keyDoc.storagePath,
  67:       signedStorageUrl,
  68:       keyB64: keyDoc.keyB64,
  69:       ivB64: keyDoc.ivB64,
  70:       signedUrlExpiresAt: expiresAt.toISOString(),
  71:     },
  72:   };
  73: };
  74: 
  75: const handler: HttpHandler = requireAdmin(['super-admin', 'ops-manager'])(adminSosPlaybackTokenHandler);
  76: 
  77: app.http('adminSosPlaybackToken', {
  78:   methods: ['GET'],
  79:   route: 'v1/admin/sos/{incidentId}/playback-token',
  80:   authLevel: 'anonymous',
  81:   handler,
  82: });

--- api/src/functions/sos-key.ts
   1: import '../bootstrap.js';
   2: import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
   3: import type { HttpHandler } from '@azure/functions';
   4: import { app } from '@azure/functions';
   5: import { requireCustomer } from '../middleware/requireCustomer.js';
   6: import type { CustomerHttpHandler } from '../middleware/requireCustomer.js';
   7: import { bookingRepo } from '../cosmos/booking-repository.js';
   8: import { putKeyDoc, getKeyDoc } from '../cosmos/sos-incident-key-repository.js';
   9: import { appendAuditEntry } from '../cosmos/audit-log-repository.js';
  10: import type { AuditLogDoc } from '../schemas/audit-log.js';
  11: import { SosKeyUploadRequest } from '../schemas/sos.js';
  12: 
  13: const sosKeyInner: CustomerHttpHandler = async (req, ctx, customer) => {
  14:   const incidentId = (req as unknown as { params: { incidentId: string } }).params.incidentId;
  15: 
  16:   let raw: unknown;
  17:   try {
  18:     raw = await req.json();
  19:   } catch {
  20:     return { status: 400, jsonBody: { code: 'INVALID_JSON' } };
  21:   }
  22: 
  23:   const parsed = SosKeyUploadRequest.safeParse(raw);
  24:   if (!parsed.success) {
  25:     return { status: 400, jsonBody: { code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR', issues: parsed.error.issues } };
  26:   }
  27: 
  28:   const { keyB64, ivB64, storagePath } = parsed.data;
  29: 
  30:   const booking = await bookingRepo.getById(incidentId);
  31:   if (!booking) return { status: 404, jsonBody: { code: 'BOOKING_NOT_FOUND' } };
  32:   if (booking.customerId !== customer.customerId) {
  33:     return { status: 403, jsonBody: { code: 'FORBIDDEN' } };
  34:   }
  35: 
  36:   if (!booking.sosActivatedAt) {
  37:     return { status: 409, jsonBody: { code: 'SOS_NOT_ACTIVATED' } };
  38:   }
  39: 
  40:   const canonicalPath = `sos-audio/${customer.customerId}/${incidentId}.enc`;
  41:   if (storagePath !== canonicalPath) {
  42:     return { status: 422, jsonBody: { code: 'INVALID_STORAGE_PATH', expected: canonicalPath } };
  43:   }
  44: 
  45:   const existing = await getKeyDoc(incidentId, customer.customerId);
  46:   if (existing) {
  47:     return { status: 200, jsonBody: { code: 'ALREADY_PROCESSED' } };
  48:   }
  49: 
  50:   await putKeyDoc({
  51:     id: incidentId,
  52:     customerId: customer.customerId,
  53:     keyB64,
  54:     ivB64,
  55:     storagePath,
  56:   });
  57: 
  58:   const now = new Date().toISOString();
  59:   const auditEntry: AuditLogDoc = {
  60:     id: randomUUID(),
  61:     adminId: customer.customerId,
  62:     role: 'system',
  63:     action: 'SOS_KEY_UPLOADED',
  64:     resourceType: 'booking',
  65:     resourceId: incidentId,
  66:     payload: { incidentId, storagePath },
  67:     timestamp: now,
  68:     partitionKey: now.slice(0, 7),
  69:   };
  70:   appendAuditEntry(auditEntry).catch((err: unknown) => ctx.error('Audit SOS_KEY_UPLOADED failed', err));
  71: 
  72:   return { status: 201 };
  73: };
  74: 
  75: export const sosKeyHandler: HttpHandler = requireCustomer(sosKeyInner);
  76: 
  77: app.http('sosKey', {
  78:   methods: ['POST'],
  79:   route: 'v1/sos/{incidentId}/key',
  80:   authLevel: 'anonymous',
  81:   handler: sosKeyHandler,
  82: });
--- api/src/cosmos/sos-incident-key-repository.ts
   1: import { getSosIncidentKeysContainer } from './client.js';
   2: import type { SosIncidentKeyDoc } from '../schemas/sos.js';
   3: 
   4: const TTL_7_DAYS = 604_800;
   5: 
   6: export async function putKeyDoc(doc: Omit<SosIncidentKeyDoc, 'ttl' | 'createdAt'>): Promise<void> {
   7:   const full: SosIncidentKeyDoc = {
   8:     ...doc,
   9:     createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  10:     ttl: TTL_7_DAYS,
  11:   };
  12:   await getSosIncidentKeysContainer().items.create<SosIncidentKeyDoc>(full);
  13: }
  14: 
  15: export async function getKeyDoc(
  16:   incidentId: string,
  17:   customerId: string,
  18: ): Promise<SosIncidentKeyDoc | null> {
  19:   const { resource } = await getSosIncidentKeysContainer()
  20:     .item(incidentId, customerId)
  21:     .read<SosIncidentKeyDoc>();
  22:   return resource ?? null;
  23: }
--- api/src/schemas/sos.ts
   1: import { z } from 'zod';
   2: 
   3: // AES-256 key = 32 bytes â†’ 44 base64 chars (43 + one '=' pad: 32 = 10*3+2 â†’ 44 chars)
   4: const KEY_B64_RE = /^[A-Za-z0-9+/]{43}=$/;
   5: // GCM IV = 12 bytes â†’ 16 base64 chars (12 = 4*3, no padding needed)
   6: const IV_B64_RE = /^[A-Za-z0-9+/]{16}$/;
   7: 
   8: export const SosIncidentKeyDoc = z.object({
   9:   id: z.string().min(1),
  10:   customerId: z.string().min(1),
  11:   keyB64: z.string().regex(KEY_B64_RE, 'keyB64 must be 32-byte AES key in base64 (44 chars, one = pad)'),
  12:   ivB64: z.string().regex(IV_B64_RE, 'ivB64 must be 12-byte GCM IV in base64 (16 chars, no pad)'),
  13:   storagePath: z.string().regex(/^sos-audio\/[^/]+\/[^/]+\.enc$/),
  14:   createdAt: z.string().datetime(),
  15:   ttl: z.number().int().positive(),
  16: });
  17: 
  18: export type SosIncidentKeyDoc = z.infer<typeof SosIncidentKeyDoc>;
  19: 
  20: export const SosKeyUploadRequest = SosIncidentKeyDoc.pick({
  21:   keyB64: true,
  22:   ivB64: true,
  23:   storagePath: true,
  24: });
  25: 
  26: export type SosKeyUploadRequest = z.infer<typeof SosKeyUploadRequest>;
--- firebase/storage.rules
   1: rules_version = '2';
   2: service firebase.storage {
   3:   match /b/{bucket}/o {
   4:     // KYC documents â€” each user owns their own subtree
   5:     match /kyc/{userId}/{allPaths=**} {
   6:       allow read, write: if request.auth != null
   7:                          && request.auth.uid == userId;
   8:     }
   9: 
  10:     // Job site photos â€” technician UID is embedded in the path so rules can
  11:     // enforce ownership without a Cosmos DB lookup.
  12:     // Path: bookings/{bookingId}/photos/{technicianUid}/{stage}/{file}
  13:     //
  14:     // Reads: restricted to the uploading technician. Admin and customer reads
  15:     // are served through the API using the Firebase Admin SDK (which bypasses
  16:     // Storage rules) to generate short-lived signed URLs â€” keeping PII off
  17:     // publicly-guessable paths.
  18:     match /bookings/{bookingId}/photos/{technicianUid}/{stage}/{file} {
  19:       allow write: if request.auth != null
  20:                    && request.auth.uid == technicianUid
  21:                    && request.resource.size <= 5 * 1024 * 1024
  22:                    && request.resource.contentType.matches('image/.*');
  23:       allow read: if request.auth != null
  24:                   && request.auth.uid == technicianUid;
  25:     }
  26: 
  27:     // SOS audio â€” customer creates encrypted blob (create-only; no overwrite).
  28:     // Transient key-POST failures are handled by retrying only the key endpoint
  29:     // (up to 3Ã— in SosAudioUploader) without re-uploading the blob, keeping the
  30:     // blob/key pair consistent. Admin reads via signed URL only.
  31:     // GCS lifecycle deletes after 7 days. ADR-0024: AES/GCM encrypted on-device.
  32:     match /sos-audio/{customerId}/{incidentId} {
  33:       allow create: if request.auth != null
  34:                     && request.auth.uid == customerId
  35:                     && request.resource.size <= 10 * 1024 * 1024
  36:                     && request.resource.contentType == 'application/octet-stream';
  37:       allow read: if request.auth != null
  38:                   && request.auth.token.role == 'admin';
  39:     }
  40:   }
  41: }

  15: import com.homeservices.customer.data.sos.SosUploadProgress
  16: import com.homeservices.customer.domain.auth.model.AuthState
  17: import com.homeservices.customer.domain.flags.FeatureFlags
  18: import com.homeservices.customer.domain.sos.SosUseCase
  19: import dagger.hilt.android.lifecycle.HiltViewModel
  20: import dagger.hilt.android.qualifiers.ApplicationContext
  21: import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
  22: import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
  23: import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
  24: import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
  25: import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
  26: import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
  27: import java.io.File
  28: import javax.inject.Inject
  29: 
  30: @HiltViewModel
  31: @Suppress("LongParameterList", "TooManyFunctions")
  32: public class SosViewModel
  33:     @Inject
  34:     constructor(
  35:         savedStateHandle: SavedStateHandle,
  36:         private val sosUseCase: SosUseCase,
  37:         private val consentStore: SosConsentStore,
  38:         @ApplicationContext private val context: Context,
  39:         private val featureFlags: FeatureFlags,
  40:         private val sessionManager: SessionManager,
  41:         private val audioUploader: SosAudioUploader,
  42:     ) : ViewModel() {
  43:         private val bookingId: String = checkNotNull(savedStateHandle["bookingId"])
  44:         private val _sosUiState = MutableStateFlow<SosUiState>(SosUiState.Idle)
  45:         public val sosUiState: StateFlow<SosUiState> = _sosUiState.asStateFlow()
  46: 
  47:         private var countdownJob: Job? = null
  48:         private var recorder: MediaRecorder? = null
  49: 
  50:         /** Set to true only when startRecording() completes successfully in this SOS session. */
  51:         private var freshRecordingCaptured = false
  52: 
  53:         public fun onSosTapped() {
  54:             viewModelScope.launch {
  55:                 val consent = consentStore.getAudioConsent()
  56:                 if (consent == null) {
  57:                     _sosUiState.value = SosUiState.ShowConsent
  58:                 } else {
  59:                     startCountdown(audioGranted = consent)
  60:                 }
  61:             }
  62:         }
  63: 
  64:         public fun onConsentResolved(granted: Boolean) {
  65:             viewModelScope.launch {
  66:                 consentStore.setAudioConsent(granted)
  67:                 startCountdown(audioGranted = granted)
  68:             }
  69:         }
  70: 
  71:         /** Dismiss the evidence-saved or evidence-error sheet after upload completes. */
  72:         public fun onDismissEvidenceResult() {
  73:             _sosUiState.value = SosUiState.SosConfirmed
  74:         }
  75: 
  76:         public fun onCancelCountdown() {
  77:             countdownJob?.cancel()
  78:             countdownJob = null
  79:             stopRecording()
  80:             wipeStaleSosFile()
  81:             _sosUiState.value = SosUiState.Idle
  82:         }
  83: 
  84:         /** Fires the SOS immediately, cancelling the countdown. Used by the "Send Now" button. */
  85:         public fun onSendNow() {
  86:             countdownJob?.cancel()
  87:             countdownJob = null
  88:             stopRecording()
  89:             viewModelScope.launch { fireSos() }
  90:         }
  91: 
  92:         /** Called by the UI after the RECORD_AUDIO OS permission dialog resolves. */
  93:         public fun onAudioPermissionResult(granted: Boolean) {
  94:             startCountdown(audioGranted = granted)
  95:         }
  96: 
  97:         private fun startCountdown(audioGranted: Boolean) {
  98:             val osPermissionGranted =
  99:                 ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(context, Manifest.permission.RECORD_AUDIO) ==
 100:                     PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
 101:             if (audioGranted && !osPermissionGranted) {
 102:                 // Pause and ask UI to request the OS permission; countdown resumes via onAudioPermissionResult.
 103:                 _sosUiState.value = SosUiState.RequestAudioPermission
 104:                 return
 105:             }
 106:             countdownJob?.cancel()
 107:             countdownJob =
 108:                 viewModelScope.launch {
 109:                     // Wipe any stale file from a crash/old build before this countdown starts.
 110:                     wipeStaleSosFile()
 111:                     freshRecordingCaptured = false
 112:                     if (audioGranted && osPermissionGranted) startRecording()
 113:                     for (sec in 30 downTo 1) {
 114:                         _sosUiState.value = SosUiState.Countdown(sec)
 115:                         delay(1_000L)
 116:                     }
 117:                     stopRecording()
 118:                     fireSos()
 119:                 }
 120:         }
 121: 
 122:         private fun startRecording() {
 123:             runCatching {
 124:                 val dir = File(context.filesDir, "sos").also { it.mkdirs() }
 125:                 val file = File(dir, "sos-$bookingId.m4a")
 126:                 val rec =
 127:                     if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
 128:                         MediaRecorder(context)
 129:                     } else {
 130:                         @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
 131:                         MediaRecorder()
 132:                     }
 133:                 rec.apply {
 134:                     setAudioSource(MediaRecorder.AudioSource.MIC)
 135:                     setOutputFormat(MediaRecorder.OutputFormat.MPEG_4)
 136:                     setAudioEncoder(MediaRecorder.AudioEncoder.AAC)
 137:                     setOutputFile(file.absolutePath)
 138:                     prepare()
 139:                     start()
 140:                 }
 141:                 recorder = rec
 142:                 freshRecordingCaptured = true
 143:             }
 144:         }
 145: 
 146:         /** For testing only: simulate a successful recording capture. */
 147:         @Suppress("unused")
 148:         internal fun simulateFreshRecordingCapturedForTest() {
 149:             freshRecordingCaptured = true
 150:         }
 151: 
 152:         private fun stopRecording() {
 153:             runCatching {
 154:                 recorder?.apply {
 155:                     stop()
 156:                     release()
 157:                 }
 158:                 recorder = null
 159:             }
 160:         }
 161: 
 162:         private suspend fun fireSos() {
 163:             val result = sosUseCase.execute(bookingId)
 164:             if (!result.isSuccess) {
 165:                 _sosUiState.value = SosUiState.SosError(result.exceptionOrNull()?.message ?: "Unknown error")
 166:                 return
 167:             }
 168:             _sosUiState.value = SosUiState.SosConfirmed
 169:             maybeUploadEvidence()
 170:         }
 171: 
 172:         private suspend fun maybeUploadEvidence() {
 173:             if (!featureFlags.sosAudioUploadEnabled() || !freshRecordingCaptured) return
 174:             val customerId = (sessionManager.authState.value as? AuthState.Authenticated)?.uid
 175:             val file = File(File(context.filesDir, "sos"), "sos-$bookingId.m4a")
 176:             if (customerId == null || !file.exists()) return
 177: 
 178:             val bytes = file.readBytes()
 179:             file.delete()
 180:             audioUploader.upload(customerId, bookingId, bytes).collect { progress ->
 181:                 _sosUiState.value =
 182:                     when (progress) {
 183:                         is SosUploadProgress.Progress -> SosUiState.UploadingEvidence(progress.pct)
 184:                         is SosUploadProgress.Success -> SosUiState.EvidenceSaved
 185:                         is SosUploadProgress.Failure ->
 186:                             SosUiState.EvidenceUploadError(progress.cause.message ?: "upload_failed")
 187:                     }
 188:             }
 189:         }
 190: 
 191:         private fun wipeStaleSosFile() {
 192:             freshRecordingCaptured = false
 193:             runCatching { File(File(context.filesDir, "sos"), "sos-$bookingId.m4a").delete() }
 194:         }
 195: 
 196:         override fun onCleared() {
 197:             super.onCleared()
 198:             countdownJob?.cancel()
 199:             stopRecording()
 200:             wipeStaleSosFile()
 201:         }
 202:     }

 103: /**

 108:  */
 109: @Composable
 110: private fun AppNavigationReady(
 111:     sessionManager: SessionManager,
 112:     activity: FragmentActivity,
 113:     pendingActionStore: PendingActionStore,
 114:     featureFlags: FeatureFlags,
 115:     firstLaunchPending: Boolean,
 116:     modifier: Modifier,

 119: ) {

+import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
+import type { HttpRequest, InvocationContext } from '@azure/functions';
+import type { AdminContext } from '../../../../src/types/admin.js';
+// AdminHttpHandler type used via test calls only
+
+vi.mock('../../../../src/cosmos/booking-repository.js', () => ({
+  bookingRepo: { getById: vi.fn() },
+}));
+vi.mock('../../../../src/cosmos/sos-incident-key-repository.js', () => ({
+  getKeyDoc: vi.fn(),
+}));
+vi.mock('../../../../src/services/adminSession.service.js', () => ({
+  getSessionById: vi.fn(),
+}));
+vi.mock('../../../../src/cosmos/audit-log-repository.js', () => ({
+  appendAuditEntry: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined),
+}));
+vi.mock('../../../../src/services/firebaseAdmin.js', () => ({
+  getFirebaseAdmin: vi.fn(),
+}));
+vi.mock('@sentry/node', () => ({ captureException: vi.fn() }));
+
+import { adminSosPlaybackTokenHandler } from '../../../../src/functions/admin/sos/playback-token.js';
+import { bookingRepo } from '../../../../src/cosmos/booking-repository.js';
+import { getKeyDoc } from '../../../../src/cosmos/sos-incident-key-repository.js';
+import { getSessionById } from '../../../../src/services/adminSession.service.js';
+import { getFirebaseAdmin } from '../../../../src/services/firebaseAdmin.js';
+import { appendAuditEntry } from '../../../../src/cosmos/audit-log-repository.js';
+import type { BookingDoc } from '../../../../src/schemas/booking.js';
+import type { SosIncidentKeyDoc } from '../../../../src/schemas/sos.js';
+
+const ctx = { log: vi.fn(), error: vi.fn() } as unknown as InvocationContext;
+
+function makeReq(incidentId = 'bk-1'): HttpRequest {
+  return {
+    params: { incidentId },
+    query: { get: () => null },
+  } as unknown as HttpRequest;
+}
+
+const mockAdmin: AdminContext = {
+  adminId: 'admin-1',
+  role: 'ops-manager',
+  sessionId: 'sess-1',
+};
+
+const freshSession = {
+  id: 'sess-1',
+  sessionId: 'sess-1',
+  adminId: 'admin-1',
+  role: 'ops-manager' as const,
+  lastActivityAt: new Date().toISOString(),
+  hardExpiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 8 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),
+  refreshTokenHash: 'h',
+  totpVerifiedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
+};
+
+const staleSession = {
+  ...freshSession,
+  totpVerifiedAt: new Date(Date.now() - 10 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),
+};
+
+const sampleBooking: BookingDoc = {
+  id: 'bk-1',
+  customerId: 'cust-1',
+  technicianId: 'tech-1',
+  status: 'IN_PROGRESS',
+  sosActivatedAt: '2026-05-17T10:00:00.000Z',
+  serviceId: 's',
+  categoryId: 'c',
+  slotDate: '2026-05-17',
+  slotWindow: '10:00-12:00',
+  addressText: '42 MG Road',
+  addressLatLng: { lat: 12.97, lng: 77.59 },
+  paymentOrderId: 'ord-1',
+  paymentId: 'pay-1',
+  paymentSignature: 'sig-1',
+  amount: 120000,
+  createdAt: '2026-05-17T04:30:00.000Z',
+};
+
+const sampleKeyDoc: SosIncidentKeyDoc = {
+  id: 'bk-1',
+  customerId: 'cust-1',
+  keyB64: 'AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA=',
+  ivB64: 'AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA',
+  storagePath: 'sos-audio/cust-1/bk-1.enc',
+  createdAt: '2026-05-17T10:00:00.000Z',
+  ttl: 604800,
+};
+
+const mockGetSignedUrl = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(['https://storage.googleapis.com/signed-url']);
+
+beforeEach(() => {
+  vi.resetAllMocks();
+  vi.mocked(bookingRepo.getById).mockResolvedValue(sampleBooking);
+  vi.mocked(getKeyDoc).mockResolvedValue(sampleKeyDoc);
+  vi.mocked(getSessionById).mockResolvedValue(freshSession);
+  vi.mocked(appendAuditEntry).mockResolvedValue(undefined);
+  mockGetSignedUrl.mockResolvedValue(['https://storage.googleapis.com/signed-url']);
+  vi.mocked(getFirebaseAdmin).mockReturnValue({
+    storage: () => ({
+      bucket: () => ({
+        file: () => ({ getSignedUrl: mockGetSignedUrl }),
+      }),
+    }),
+  } as unknown as ReturnType<typeof getFirebaseAdmin>);
+});
+
+describe('GET /v1/admin/sos/{incidentId}/playback-token', () => {
+  it('returns_token_for_authorized_admin_with_fresh_totp', async () => {
+    const res = await adminSosPlaybackTokenHandler(makeReq(), ctx, mockAdmin);
+    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
+    const body = res.jsonBody as any;
+    expect(body.incidentId).toBe('bk-1');
+    expect(body.keyB64).toBe(sampleKeyDoc.keyB64);
+    expect(body.ivB64).toBe(sampleKeyDoc.ivB64);
+    expect(body.signedStorageUrl).toBeTruthy();
+    expect(body.signedUrlExpiresAt).toBeTruthy();
+  });
+
+  it('rejects_when_totp_session_stale_more_than_5_min', async () => {
+    vi.mocked(getSessionById).mockResolvedValue(staleSession);
+    const res = await adminSosPlaybackTokenHandler(makeReq(), ctx, mockAdmin);
+    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
+    expect((res.jsonBody as any).code).toBe('TOTP_REFRESH_REQUIRED');
+  });
+
+  it('rejects_when_admin_role_is_finance_only', async () => {
+    const financeAdmin: AdminContext = { ...mockAdmin, role: 'finance' };
+    const res = await adminSosPlaybackTokenHandler(makeReq(), ctx, financeAdmin);
+    expect(res.status).toBe(403);
+    expect((res.jsonBody as any).code).toBe('FORBIDDEN');
+  });
+
+  it('returns_key_not_found_when_audio_was_never_uploaded', async () => {
+    vi.mocked(getKeyDoc).mockResolvedValue(null);
+    const res = await adminSosPlaybackTokenHandler(makeReq(), ctx, mockAdmin);
+    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
+    expect((res.jsonBody as any).code).toBe('KEY_NOT_FOUND');
+  });
+});
diff --git a/api/tests/functions/sos-key.test.ts b/api/tests/functions/sos-key.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..cc0ea2e5
--- /dev/null
+++ b/api/tests/functions/sos-key.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,121 @@
+import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
+import type { HttpRequest, HttpResponseInit, InvocationContext } from '@azure/functions';
+
+vi.mock('../../src/services/firebaseAdmin.js', () => ({
+  verifyFirebaseIdToken: vi.fn(),
+}));
+vi.mock('../../src/cosmos/booking-repository.js', () => ({
+  bookingRepo: { getById: vi.fn() },
+}));
+vi.mock('../../src/cosmos/sos-incident-key-repository.js', () => ({
+  putKeyDoc: vi.fn(),
+  getKeyDoc: vi.fn(),
+}));
+vi.mock('../../src/cosmos/audit-log-repository.js', () => ({
+  appendAuditEntry: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined),
+}));
+vi.mock('@sentry/node', () => ({ captureException: vi.fn() }));
+
+import { sosKeyHandler } from '../../src/functions/sos-key.js';
+import { verifyFirebaseIdToken } from '../../src/services/firebaseAdmin.js';
+import { bookingRepo } from '../../src/cosmos/booking-repository.js';
+import { putKeyDoc, getKeyDoc } from '../../src/cosmos/sos-incident-key-repository.js';
+import { appendAuditEntry } from '../../src/cosmos/audit-log-repository.js';
+import type { BookingDoc } from '../../src/schemas/booking.js';
+
+const ctx = { log: vi.fn(), error: vi.fn() } as unknown as InvocationContext;
+
+function makeReq(opts: {
+  auth?: string;
+  incidentId?: string;
+  body?: unknown;
+}): HttpRequest {
+  return {
+    headers: { get: (h: string) => (h.toLowerCase() === 'authorization' ? (opts.auth ?? '') : null) },
+    params: { incidentId: opts.incidentId ?? 'bk-1' },
+    json: async () => opts.body ?? validBody,
+  } as unknown as HttpRequest;
+}
+
+const validBody = {
+  keyB64: 'AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA=',
+  ivB64: 'AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA',
+  storagePath: 'sos-audio/cust-1/bk-1.enc',
+};
+
+const activeBooking: BookingDoc = {
+  id: 'bk-1',
+  customerId: 'cust-1',
+  technicianId: 'tech-1',
+  status: 'IN_PROGRESS',
+  sosActivatedAt: '2026-05-17T10:00:00.000Z',
+  serviceId: 's',
+  categoryId: 'c',
+  slotDate: '2026-05-17',
+  slotWindow: '10:00-12:00',
+  addressText: '42 MG Road',
+  addressLatLng: { lat: 12.97, lng: 77.59 },
+  paymentOrderId: 'ord-1',
+  paymentId: 'pay-1',
+  paymentSignature: 'sig-1',
+  amount: 120000,
+  createdAt: '2026-05-17T04:30:00.000Z',
+};
+
+beforeEach(() => {
+  vi.resetAllMocks();
+  vi.mocked(verifyFirebaseIdToken).mockResolvedValue({ uid: 'cust-1' } as any);
+  vi.mocked(bookingRepo.getById).mockResolvedValue(activeBooking);
+  vi.mocked(getKeyDoc).mockResolvedValue(null);
+  vi.mocked(putKeyDoc).mockResolvedValue(undefined);
+  vi.mocked(appendAuditEntry).mockResolvedValue(undefined);
+});
+
+describe('POST /v1/sos/{incidentId}/key', () => {
+  it('posts_key_doc_when_authenticated_owner_with_active_sos', async () => {
+    const res = await sosKeyHandler(makeReq({ auth: 'Bearer tok' }), ctx) as HttpResponseInit;
+    expect(res.status).toBe(201);
+    expect(putKeyDoc).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
+      expect.objectContaining({
+        id: 'bk-1',
+        customerId: 'cust-1',
+        keyB64: validBody.keyB64,
+        ivB64: validBody.ivB64,
+        storagePath: validBody.storagePath,
+      }),
+    );
+  });
+
+  it('rejects_when_booking_not_in_progress_or_sos_not_activated', async () => {
+    vi.mocked(bookingRepo.getById).mockResolvedValue({
+      ...activeBooking,
+      sosActivatedAt: undefined,
+    });
+    const res = await sosKeyHandler(makeReq({ auth: 'Bearer tok' }), ctx) as HttpResponseInit;
+    expect(res.status).toBe(409);
+    expect((res.jsonBody as any).code).toBe('SOS_NOT_ACTIVATED');
+  });
+
+  it('rejects_when_customerId_mismatch', async () => {
+    vi.mocked(verifyFirebaseIdToken).mockResolvedValue({ uid: 'other-cust' } as any);
+    const res = await sosKeyHandler(makeReq({ auth: 'Bearer tok' }), ctx) as HttpResponseInit;
+    expect(res.status).toBe(403);
+    expect((res.jsonBody as any).code).toBe('FORBIDDEN');
+  });
+
+  it('returns_already_processed_on_second_upload_same_incident', async () => {
+    vi.mocked(getKeyDoc).mockResolvedValue({
+      id: 'bk-1',
+      customerId: 'cust-1',
+      keyB64: validBody.keyB64,
+      ivB64: validBody.ivB64,
+      storagePath: validBody.storagePath,
+      createdAt: '2026-05-17T10:00:00.000Z',
+      ttl: 604800,
+    });
+    const res = await sosKeyHandler(makeReq({ auth: 'Bearer tok' }), ctx) as HttpResponseInit;
+    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
+    expect((res.jsonBody as any).code).toBe('ALREADY_PROCESSED');
+    expect(putKeyDoc).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+});
diff --git a/api/tests/integration/admin-routes-unauth.test.ts b/api/tests/integration/admin-routes-unauth.test.ts
index ebbffce2..b55f5ac8 100644
--- a/api/tests/integration/admin-routes-unauth.test.ts
+++ b/api/tests/integration/admin-routes-unauth.test.ts
@@ -1,147 +1,175 @@
 /**
  * admin-routes-unauth.test.ts
  *
  * Integration gate: every /v1/admin/* handler must reject unauthenticated
  * requests with HTTP 401. If a new handler ships without requireAdmin wrapping,
  * its test case here will fail, catching the regression before CI merges.
  *
  * Strategy: import each handler directly, call it with a fake HttpRequest that
  * has no Authorization header / no hs_access cookie, assert status === 401.
  */
 import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
 import { HttpRequest } from '@azure/functions';
 import type { HttpResponseInit } from '@azure/functions';
 
 process.env.JWT_SECRET = 'test-secret-that-is-long-enough-for-hs256-minimum-32-chars!!';
 
 // ── Module mocks (prevent real Cosmos / Firebase calls) ───────────────────
 vi.mock('../../src/services/adminSession.service.js', () => ({
   touchAndGetSession: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(null),
   createAdminSession: vi.fn(),
   deleteSession: vi.fn(),
   deleteAllSessionsForAdmin: vi.fn(),
+  getSessionById: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(null),
 }));
 vi.mock('../../src/services/adminUser.service.js', () => ({
   getAdminUserById: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(null),
   getAdminUserByEmail: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(null),
   isAdminInvite: vi.fn().mockReturnValue(false),
   claimAdminInvite: vi.fn(),
   updateAdminUser: vi.fn(),
 }));
 vi.mock('../../src/services/firebaseAdmin.js', () => ({
   verifyFirebaseIdToken: vi.fn().mockRejectedValue(new Error('firebase-mock-rejected')),
 }));
 vi.mock('../../src/services/auditLog.service.js', () => ({
   auditLog: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined),
 }));
 vi.mock('../../src/cosmos/audit-log-repository.js', () => ({
   appendAuditEntry: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined),
   queryAuditLog: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ entries: [] }),
 }));
 vi.mock('../../src/cosmos/orders-repository.js', () => ({
   queryOrders: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ orders: [] }),
   getOrderById: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(null),
 }));
+vi.mock('../../src/cosmos/booking-repository.js', () => ({
+  bookingRepo: { getById: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(null) },
+}));
+vi.mock('../../src/cosmos/sos-incident-key-repository.js', () => ({
+  getKeyDoc: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(null),
+  putKeyDoc: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined),
+}));
 vi.mock('../../src/cosmos/client.js', () => ({
   getCosmosClient: () => ({ database: () => ({ container: () => ({ items: { query: () => ({ fetchAll: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ resources: [] }) }) } }) }) }),
   DB_NAME: 'homeservices',
 }));
 vi.mock('../../src/services/jwt.service.js', async (importOriginal) => {
   // Keep real implementation for signing; just ensure verifyAccessToken rejects bad tokens
   const real = await importOriginal<typeof import('../../src/services/jwt.service.js')>();
   return real;
 });
 
 // ── Imports (after mocks are established) ─────────────────────────────────
 import { adminAuditLogListHandler } from '../../src/functions/admin/audit-log/list.js';
 import { adminPatchUserHandler } from '../../src/functions/admin/users/patch.js';
 import { summaryHandler } from '../../src/functions/admin/dashboard/summary.js';
 import { adminListOrdersHandler } from '../../src/functions/admin/orders/list.js';
 import { adminGetOrderHandler } from '../../src/functions/admin/orders/detail.js';
+import { adminSosPlaybackTokenHandler } from '../../src/functions/admin/sos/playback-token.js';
+import { adminGetSosIncidentHandler } from '../../src/functions/admin/sos/get-incident.js';
 import { requireAdmin } from '../../src/middleware/requireAdmin.js';
 
 const fakeCtx = {} as any;
 
 /** Bare request with no cookies, no Authorization header */
 function unauthReq(url = 'http://localhost/api/v1/admin/test', method = 'GET'): HttpRequest {
   return new HttpRequest({ url, method });
 }
 
 /** Wrap a raw handler through requireAdmin the same way production does */
 function wrapWithRequireAdmin(handler: Parameters<ReturnType<typeof requireAdmin>>[0]) {
   return requireAdmin(['super-admin', 'ops-manager', 'finance', 'support-agent'])(handler);
 }
 
 describe('Admin routes — unauthenticated requests must return 401', () => {
   it('GET /v1/admin/audit-log → 401 without auth cookie', async () => {
     const res = await adminAuditLogListHandler(
       unauthReq('http://localhost/api/v1/admin/audit-log'),
       fakeCtx,
     ) as HttpResponseInit;
     expect(res.status).toBe(401);
   });
 
   it('PATCH /v1/admin/users/{adminId} → 401 without auth cookie', async () => {
     const wrapped = wrapWithRequireAdmin(adminPatchUserHandler);
     const req = new HttpRequest({
       url: 'http://localhost/api/v1/admin/users/some-id',
       method: 'PATCH',
     });
     const res = await wrapped(req, fakeCtx) as HttpResponseInit;
     expect(res.status).toBe(401);
   });
 
   it('GET /v1/admin/dashboard/summary → 401 without auth cookie', async () => {
     const wrapped = wrapWithRequireAdmin(summaryHandler);
     const res = await wrapped(
       unauthReq('http://localhost/api/v1/admin/dashboard/summary'),
       fakeCtx,
     ) as HttpResponseInit;
     expect(res.status).toBe(401);
   });
 
   it('GET /v1/admin/orders → 401 without auth cookie', async () => {
     const wrapped = wrapWithRequireAdmin(adminListOrdersHandler);
     const res = await wrapped(
       unauthReq('http://localhost/api/v1/admin/orders'),
       fakeCtx,
     ) as HttpResponseInit;
     expect(res.status).toBe(401);
   });
 
   it('GET /v1/admin/orders/{id} → 401 without auth cookie', async () => {
     const wrapped = wrapWithRequireAdmin(adminGetOrderHandler);
     const res = await wrapped(
       unauthReq('http://localhost/api/v1/admin/orders/booking-123'),
       fakeCtx,
     ) as HttpResponseInit;
     expect(res.status).toBe(401);
   });
 
+  it('GET /v1/admin/sos/{incidentId}/playback-token → 401 without auth cookie', async () => {
+    const wrapped = wrapWithRequireAdmin(adminSosPlaybackTokenHandler);
+    const res = await wrapped(
+      unauthReq('http://localhost/api/v1/admin/sos/bk-1/playback-token'),
+      fakeCtx,
+    ) as HttpResponseInit;
+    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
+  });
+
+  it('GET /v1/admin/sos/{incidentId} → 401 without auth cookie', async () => {
+    const wrapped = wrapWithRequireAdmin(adminGetSosIncidentHandler);
+    const res = await wrapped(
+      unauthReq('http://localhost/api/v1/admin/sos/bk-1'),
+      fakeCtx,
+    ) as HttpResponseInit;
+    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
+  });
+
   it('401 response includes UNAUTHENTICATED code', async () => {
     const wrapped = wrapWithRequireAdmin(summaryHandler);
     const res = await wrapped(
       unauthReq('http://localhost/api/v1/admin/dashboard/summary'),
       fakeCtx,
     ) as HttpResponseInit;
     expect((res.jsonBody as any)?.code).toBe('UNAUTHENTICATED');
   });
 });
 
 describe('Admin login route — no requireAdmin wrapping (public endpoint)', () => {
   it('POST /v1/admin/auth/login returns non-401 for missing body (validation error, not auth error)', async () => {
     // Login is a PUBLIC endpoint — it should NOT return 401 for missing credentials.
     // It should return 400 (validation) or 401 (firebase token invalid), but NOT
     // because of a missing requireAdmin gate.
     // We verify it doesn't crash and returns a meaningful error code.
     const { adminLoginHandler } = await import('../../src/functions/admin/auth/login.js');
     const req = new HttpRequest({
       url: 'http://localhost/api/v1/admin/auth/login',
       method: 'POST',
       body: { string: JSON.stringify({}) },
     });
     const res = await adminLoginHandler(req, fakeCtx) as HttpResponseInit;
     // Should be 400 (invalid request body, not enough fields) — NOT caused by requireAdmin
     expect([400, 422]).toContain(res.status);
   });
 });
diff --git a/api/tests/services/fcm.service.test.ts b/api/tests/services/fcm.service.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..8e2ccd9d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/api/tests/services/fcm.service.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,35 @@
+import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
+
+vi.mock('../../src/services/firebaseAdmin.js', () => ({
+  getFirebaseAdmin: vi.fn(),
+}));
+
+import { sendOwnerSosAlert } from '../../src/services/fcm.service.js';
+import { getFirebaseAdmin } from '../../src/services/firebaseAdmin.js';
+
+const mockSend = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue('msg-id');
+
+beforeEach(() => {
+  vi.clearAllMocks();
+  vi.mocked(getFirebaseAdmin).mockReturnValue({
+    messaging: () => ({ send: mockSend }),
+  } as any);
+});
+
+describe('sendOwnerSosAlert', () => {
+  it('sendOwnerSosAlert_does_not_leak_address_in_fcm_payload', async () => {
+    await sendOwnerSosAlert({
+      bookingId: 'bk-1',
+      customerId: 'cust-1',
+      technicianId: 'tech-1',
+      incidentId: 'bk-1',
+    });
+
+    expect(mockSend).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
+    const firstCall = mockSend.mock.calls[0]!;
+    const message = firstCall[0] as { data: Record<string, string>; topic: string };
+    expect(message.data).not.toHaveProperty('slotAddress');
+    expect(message.data.type).toBe('SOS_ALERT');
+    expect(message.data.incidentId).toBe('bk-1');
+  });
+});
diff --git a/api/tests/unit/sos.test.ts b/api/tests/unit/sos.test.ts
index 762d9a7e..f1dedf46 100644
--- a/api/tests/unit/sos.test.ts
+++ b/api/tests/unit/sos.test.ts
@@ -28,112 +28,112 @@ const ctx = { log: vi.fn(), error: vi.fn() } as unknown as InvocationContext;
 function makeReq(opts: { auth?: string; bookingId?: string }): HttpRequest {
   return {
     headers: { get: (h: string) => (h.toLowerCase() === 'authorization' ? (opts.auth ?? '') : null) },
     params: { bookingId: opts.bookingId ?? 'bk-1' },
   } as unknown as HttpRequest;
 }
 
 const inProgressBooking: BookingDoc = {
   id: 'bk-1', customerId: 'cust-1', technicianId: 'tech-1', status: 'IN_PROGRESS',
   serviceId: 's', categoryId: 'c', slotDate: '2026-04-26', slotWindow: '10:00-12:00',
   addressText: '42 MG Road, Bangalore', addressLatLng: { lat: 12.97, lng: 77.59 },
   paymentOrderId: 'ord-1', paymentId: 'pay-1', paymentSignature: 'sig-1',
   amount: 120000, createdAt: '2026-04-26T04:30:00.000Z',
 };
 
 beforeEach(() => {
   vi.resetAllMocks();
   vi.mocked(verifyFirebaseIdToken).mockResolvedValue({ uid: 'cust-1' } as any);
   vi.mocked(bookingRepo.getById).mockResolvedValue(inProgressBooking);
   vi.mocked(bookingRepo.markSosActivated).mockResolvedValue({ ...inProgressBooking, sosActivatedAt: '2026-04-26T06:00:00.000Z' });
   vi.mocked(sendOwnerSosAlert).mockResolvedValue(undefined);
   vi.mocked(appendAuditEntry).mockResolvedValue(undefined);
   vi.mocked(updateBookingFields).mockResolvedValue(undefined as any);
 });
 
 describe('POST /v1/sos/{bookingId}', () => {
   it('returns 401 when no Authorization header', async () => {
     const res = await sosHandler(makeReq({}), ctx) as HttpResponseInit;
     expect(res.status).toBe(401);
   });
 
   it('returns 404 when booking not found', async () => {
     vi.mocked(bookingRepo.getById).mockResolvedValue(null);
     const res = await sosHandler(makeReq({ auth: 'Bearer tok', bookingId: 'bk-x' }), ctx) as HttpResponseInit;
     expect(res.status).toBe(404);
   });
 
   it('returns 403 when caller is not the booking customer', async () => {
     vi.mocked(verifyFirebaseIdToken).mockResolvedValue({ uid: 'other-cust' } as any);
     const res = await sosHandler(makeReq({ auth: 'Bearer tok' }), ctx) as HttpResponseInit;
     expect(res.status).toBe(403);
   });
 
   it('returns 409 BOOKING_NOT_IN_PROGRESS when status is not IN_PROGRESS', async () => {
     vi.mocked(bookingRepo.getById).mockResolvedValue({ ...inProgressBooking, status: 'ASSIGNED' });
     const res = await sosHandler(makeReq({ auth: 'Bearer tok' }), ctx) as HttpResponseInit;
     expect(res.status).toBe(409);
     expect((res.jsonBody as any).code).toBe('BOOKING_NOT_IN_PROGRESS');
   });
 
   it('returns 200 ALREADY_PROCESSED when both sosActivatedAt and sosAlertSentAt are set', async () => {
     vi.mocked(bookingRepo.getById).mockResolvedValue({
       ...inProgressBooking,
       sosActivatedAt: '2026-04-26T05:00:00.000Z',
       sosAlertSentAt: '2026-04-26T05:00:01.000Z',
     });
     const res = await sosHandler(makeReq({ auth: 'Bearer tok' }), ctx) as HttpResponseInit;
     expect(res.status).toBe(200);
     expect((res.jsonBody as any).code).toBe('ALREADY_PROCESSED');
     expect(bookingRepo.markSosActivated).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
     expect(sendOwnerSosAlert).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
   });
 
   it('retries FCM when sosActivatedAt is set but sosAlertSentAt is absent (prior run FCM failure)', async () => {
     vi.mocked(bookingRepo.getById).mockResolvedValue({
       ...inProgressBooking,
       sosActivatedAt: '2026-04-26T05:00:00.000Z',
     });
     const res = await sosHandler(makeReq({ auth: 'Bearer tok' }), ctx) as HttpResponseInit;
     expect(res.status).toBe(201);
     expect(bookingRepo.markSosActivated).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
     expect(sendOwnerSosAlert).toHaveBeenCalled();
     expect(updateBookingFields).toHaveBeenCalledWith('bk-1', expect.objectContaining({ sosAlertSentAt: expect.any(String) }));
   });
 
   it('returns 201 on fresh SOS — marks first, sends alert, writes sosAlertSentAt', async () => {
     const res = await sosHandler(makeReq({ auth: 'Bearer tok' }), ctx) as HttpResponseInit;
     expect(res.status).toBe(201);
     expect(bookingRepo.markSosActivated).toHaveBeenCalledWith('bk-1');
     expect(sendOwnerSosAlert).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
-      bookingId: 'bk-1', customerId: 'cust-1', technicianId: 'tech-1', slotAddress: '42 MG Road, Bangalore',
+      bookingId: 'bk-1', customerId: 'cust-1', technicianId: 'tech-1', incidentId: 'bk-1',
     });
     expect(updateBookingFields).toHaveBeenCalledWith('bk-1', expect.objectContaining({ sosAlertSentAt: expect.any(String) }));
     expect(appendAuditEntry).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({
       action: 'SOS_TRIGGERED', resourceType: 'booking', resourceId: 'bk-1', role: 'system',
     }));
   });
 
   it('FCM failure propagates — sosAlertSentAt is not written so next retry can resend', async () => {
     vi.mocked(sendOwnerSosAlert).mockRejectedValue(new Error('FCM down'));
     await expect(sosHandler(makeReq({ auth: 'Bearer tok' }), ctx)).rejects.toThrow('FCM down');
     expect(bookingRepo.markSosActivated).toHaveBeenCalled();
     expect(updateBookingFields).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
   });
 
   it('FCM payload uses empty string when technicianId is absent', async () => {
     vi.mocked(bookingRepo.getById).mockResolvedValue({ ...inProgressBooking, technicianId: undefined });
     const res = await sosHandler(makeReq({ auth: 'Bearer tok' }), ctx) as HttpResponseInit;
     expect(res.status).toBe(201);
     expect(sendOwnerSosAlert).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
       expect.objectContaining({ technicianId: '' }),
     );
   });
 
   it('returns 200 ALREADY_PROCESSED when markSosActivated returns null (ETag race lost)', async () => {
     vi.mocked(bookingRepo.markSosActivated).mockResolvedValue(null);
     const res = await sosHandler(makeReq({ auth: 'Bearer tok' }), ctx) as HttpResponseInit;
     expect(res.status).toBe(200);
     expect((res.jsonBody as any).code).toBe('ALREADY_PROCESSED');
     expect(sendOwnerSosAlert).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
   });
 });
diff --git a/customer-app/app/src/test/kotlin/com/homeservices/customer/data/sos/SosAudioCipherTest.kt b/customer-app/app/src/test/kotlin/com/homeservices/customer/data/sos/SosAudioCipherTest.kt
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..40f0a52a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/customer-app/app/src/test/kotlin/com/homeservices/customer/data/sos/SosAudioCipherTest.kt
@@ -0,0 +1,46 @@
+package com.homeservices.customer.data.sos
+
+import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
+import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test
+import java.util.Base64
+import javax.crypto.Cipher
+import javax.crypto.spec.GCMParameterSpec
+import javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec
+
+public class SosAudioCipherTest {
+    private val cipher = SosAudioCipher()
+
+    @Test
+    public fun `encrypt_then_decrypt_roundtrip_matches_original_bytes`() {
+        val original = "hello sos audio".toByteArray()
+        val blob = cipher.encrypt(original.copyOf())
+
+        val keyBytes = Base64.getDecoder().decode(blob.keyB64)
+        val iv = Base64.getDecoder().decode(blob.ivB64)
+        val secretKey = SecretKeySpec(keyBytes, "AES")
+        val c = Cipher.getInstance("AES/GCM/NoPadding")
+        c.init(Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE, secretKey, GCMParameterSpec(128, iv))
+        val decrypted = c.doFinal(blob.ciphertext)
+
+        assertThat(decrypted).isEqualTo(original)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    public fun `each_invocation_produces_unique_key_and_iv`() {
+        val plain = ByteArray(64) { it.toByte() }
+        val blob1 = cipher.encrypt(plain.copyOf())
+        val blob2 = cipher.encrypt(plain.copyOf())
+
+        assertThat(blob1.keyB64).isNotEqualTo(blob2.keyB64)
+        assertThat(blob1.ivB64).isNotEqualTo(blob2.ivB64)
+        assertThat(blob1.ciphertext).isNotEqualTo(blob2.ciphertext)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    public fun `ciphertext_length_equals_plaintext_length_plus_16_byte_tag`() {
+        val plain = ByteArray(100) { 0xAB.toByte() }
+        val blob = cipher.encrypt(plain.copyOf())
+
+        assertThat(blob.ciphertext.size).isEqualTo(plain.size + 16)
+    }
+}

     public fun `onSosTapped emits ShowConsent when consent not yet answered`(): Unit =
         runTest(testDispatcher) {
             coEvery { consentStore.getAudioConsent() } returns null
             val vm = buildVm()
             vm.onSosTapped()
             advanceUntilIdle()
             assertThat(vm.sosUiState.value).isInstanceOf(SosUiState.ShowConsent::class.java)
         }
 
     @Test
     public fun `onSosTapped skips consent and emits Countdown on first tick`(): Unit =
         runTest(testDispatcher) {
             coEvery { consentStore.getAudioConsent() } returns false
             val vm = buildVm()
             vm.onSosTapped()
             advanceTimeBy(1L) // processes launch + Countdown(30) emit, stops at delay(1s)
             assertThat(vm.sosUiState.value).isInstanceOf(SosUiState.Countdown::class.java)
             assertThat((vm.sosUiState.value as SosUiState.Countdown).secondsLeft).isEqualTo(30)
             // Cancel before test-scope cleanup to avoid mock-missing fireSos call
             vm.onCancelCountdown()
         }
 
     @Test

         runTest(testDispatcher) {
             coEvery { consentStore.getAudioConsent() } returns null
             coEvery { consentStore.setAudioConsent(false) } returns Unit
             val vm = buildVm()
             vm.onSosTapped()
             advanceUntilIdle()
             vm.onConsentResolved(false)
             advanceTimeBy(1L)
             coVerify { consentStore.setAudioConsent(false) }
             assertThat(vm.sosUiState.value).isInstanceOf(SosUiState.Countdown::class.java)
             vm.onCancelCountdown()
         }

         runTest(testDispatcher) {
             coEvery { consentStore.getAudioConsent() } returns false
             val vm = buildVm()
             vm.onSosTapped()
             advanceTimeBy(1L)
             vm.onCancelCountdown()
             assertThat(vm.sosUiState.value).isInstanceOf(SosUiState.Idle::class.java)
         }

             coEvery { sosUseCase.execute("bk-1") } returns Result.success(Unit)
             val vm = buildVm()
             vm.onSosTapped()
             advanceTimeBy(31_000L)
             advanceUntilIdle()
             assertThat(vm.sosUiState.value).isInstanceOf(SosUiState.SosConfirmed::class.java)
             coVerify { sosUseCase.execute("bk-1") }
         }
 
     @Test

         runTest(testDispatcher) {
             coEvery { consentStore.getAudioConsent() } returns false
             coEvery { sosUseCase.execute("bk-1") } returns Result.failure(RuntimeException("network"))
             val vm = buildVm()
             vm.onSosTapped()
             advanceTimeBy(31_000L)

             assertThat(vm.sosUiState.value).isInstanceOf(SosUiState.SosError::class.java)
         }

         runTest(testDispatcher) {

         }

         runTest(testDispatcher) {

             val vm = buildVm()
             vm.onSosTapped()
             advanceTimeBy(1L) // countdown at 30s
             vm.onSendNow()
             advanceUntilIdle()
             assertThat(vm.sosUiState.value).isInstanceOf(SosUiState.SosConfirmed::class.java)
             coVerify(exactly = 1) { sosUseCase.execute("bk-1") }
         }

             unmockkStatic(ContextCompat::class)
         }

admin-web\README.md:1:# HomeHeroo admin
admin-web\README.md:3:Owner-facing admin dashboard for the HomeHeroo platform. Next.js 15 (App Router, RSC-first) + TypeScript strict + Tailwind v4 + Storybook. Hosted on Azure Static Web Apps free tier (deploy story is separate).
admin-web\README.md:47:pnpm test:e2e             # Playwright (Chromium); boots api/ on :7071 + admin-web on :3000
admin-web\README.md:72:- **Codex CLI is the authoritative review gate.** `.codex-review-passed` marker required before push (`.claude/settings.json` enforces it locally; `.github/workflows/admin-ship.yml` enforces it in CI via the ancestor-check pattern).
admin-web\README.md:82:- **Base URL:** set `API_BASE_URL` (server-only, never `NEXT_PUBLIC_*`) or default to `http://localhost:7071/api` locally and `https://func-homeservices-prod.azurewebsites.net/api` in production. Browser calls default to the same-origin `/admin-api/*` proxy; leave `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL` unset unless you intentionally want to bypass that proxy.

admin-web\README.md:1:# HomeHeroo admin
admin-web\README.md:3:Owner-facing admin dashboard for the HomeHeroo platform. Next.js 15 (App Router, RSC-first) + TypeScript strict + Tailwind v4 + Storybook. Hosted on Azure Static Web Apps free tier (deploy story is separate).
admin-web\README.md:47:pnpm test:e2e             # Playwright (Chromium); boots api/ on :7071 + admin-web on :3000
admin-web\README.md:72:- **Codex CLI is the authoritative review gate.** `.codex-review-passed` marker required before push (`.claude/settings.json` enforces it locally; `.github/workflows/admin-ship.yml` enforces it in CI via the ancestor-check pattern).
admin-web\README.md:82:- **Base URL:** set `API_BASE_URL` (server-only, never `NEXT_PUBLIC_*`) or default to `http://localhost:7071/api` locally and `https://func-homeservices-prod.azurewebsites.net/api` in production. Browser calls default to the same-origin `/admin-api/*` proxy; leave `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL` unset unless you intentionally want to bypass that proxy.

   1: import type { HttpHandler, HttpRequest, InvocationContext, HttpResponseInit } from '@azure/functions';
   2: import { parseCookies } from '../shared/cookies.js';
   3: import { verifyAccessToken } from '../services/jwt.service.js';
   4: import { touchAndGetSession } from '../services/adminSession.service.js';
   5: import { normalizeAdminRole, type AdminRole, type AdminContext } from '../types/admin.js';
   6: 
   7: export type AdminHttpHandler = (
   8:   req: HttpRequest,
   9:   ctx: InvocationContext,
  10:   admin: AdminContext,
  11: ) => Promise<HttpResponseInit>;
  12: 
  13: export function requireAdmin(roles: AdminRole[]) {
  14:   return (handler: AdminHttpHandler): HttpHandler =>
  15:     async (req: HttpRequest, ctx: InvocationContext): Promise<HttpResponseInit> => {
  16:       const cookies = parseCookies(req.headers.get('cookie') ?? undefined);
  17:       const token = cookies['hs_access'];
  18:       if (!token) return { status: 401, jsonBody: { code: 'UNAUTHENTICATED' } };
  19: 
  20:       const payload = await verifyAccessToken(token);
  21:       if (!payload) return { status: 401, jsonBody: { code: 'TOKEN_INVALID' } };
  22: 
  23:       const session = await touchAndGetSession(payload.sessionId);
  24:       if (!session) return { status: 401, jsonBody: { code: 'SESSION_EXPIRED' } };
  25: 
  26:       const role = normalizeAdminRole(payload.role);
  27:       if (!role) return { status: 401, jsonBody: { code: 'TOKEN_INVALID' } };
  28: 
  29:       if (!roles.includes(role)) {
  30:         return { status: 403, jsonBody: { code: 'FORBIDDEN', requiredRoles: roles } };
  31:       }
  32: 
  33:       return handler(req, ctx, {
  34:         adminId: payload.sub,
  35:         role,
  36:         sessionId: payload.sessionId,
  37:       });
  38:     };
  39: }
--- admin types
   1: export type AdminRole =
   2:   | 'super-admin'
   3:   | 'ops-manager'
   4:   | 'finance'
   5:   | 'support-agent'
   6:   | 'system';
   7: 
   8: export function normalizeAdminRole(role: unknown): AdminRole | null {
   9:   if (role === 'admin') return 'super-admin';
  10:   if (
  11:     role === 'super-admin' ||
  12:     role === 'ops-manager' ||
  13:     role === 'finance' ||
  14:     role === 'support-agent' ||
  15:     role === 'system'
  16:   ) {
  17:     return role;
  18:   }
  19:   return null;
  20: }
  21:
